# Evo X ECU 55570306 Table Explanation Guide

Public-facing description guide for the completed `55570306_FULL_CORRECTED.xml` Mitsubishi Lancer Evolution X 5MT definition.

This guide intentionally does not list ROM memory locations, raw hex locations, or internal code locations. It explains what each discovered top-level table/map/threshold/parameter is for, grouped by XML category, so it can be shared with customers without publishing the location list.

## ROM / Definition Summary

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| ROM ID | `55570306` |
| Make | `Mitsubishi` |
| Market | `USDM` |
| Model | `Lancer Evolution X` |
| Year | `2010` |
| Transmission | `5MT` |
| Flash method | `mitsucan` |
| Memory model | `M32186F8` |
| Checksum module | `mitsucan` |

## Reading Notes

- `1D` entries are scalar parameters, switches, thresholds, or compact calibration values.
- `2D` entries are single-axis curves.
- `3D` entries are two-axis maps/surfaces.
- Axis labels are shown only as signal names, element counts, and scaling names; memory locations are intentionally omitted.
- DTC and diagnostic tables should be treated carefully. A definition entry being present does not mean it should be disabled or modified without confirming the hardware and monitor behavior.

| 1786 | Reactive Wastegate Maximum Positive Correction | 2D | `OffsetWGDC` | Y Axis: Throttle Position - Target Shared Axis (9 points) (9 pts, Throttle %) | Defines a limit, clamp, or threshold used by wastegate duty command and compensation. In the XML this is represented as “Reactive Wastegate Maximum Positive Correction.” Values are selected along one axis, so it normally shapes a curve across that operating input. | Lookup-table entry from the completed M32R definition pass. |
| 1787 | Wastegate Duty Initialization or Default Value | 1D | `NativeUnsigned16` | single value / compact parameter | Sets a default, selection boundary, or operating-state transition value for wastegate duty command and compensation. In the XML this is represented as “Wastegate Duty Initialization or Default Value.” Treat it as a scalar/small parameter unless the scaling indicates a packed value. | Code-referenced calibration entry from the completed definition pass. |
